';' : ','; const header = lines[0]?.toLowerCase().split(sep).map(c=>c.trim().replace(/"/g,'')); const idxDesc = header?.findIndex(c => /descri|memo|histor|lança/i.test(c)) ?? -1; const idxVal = header?.findIndex(c => /valor|amount|debit|credit/i.test(c)) ?? -1;
